          <span class="countdown_number" id="days">30</span>
          <div class="countdown_divider"></div>
          <span class="countdown_label">DAYS</span>
        </div>
        <div class="countdown_column">

        </div>
        <div class="countdown_column">

          <span class="countdown_label">MINUTES</span>
        </div>
        <div class="countdown_column">
          <span class="countdown_number" id="seconds">59</span>

  <script>
    // Live Countdown Timer logic
    // Target date: 30 days from now
    const targetDate = new Date();
    targetDate.setDate(targetDate.getDate() + 30);

    function updateCountdown() {
      const now = new Date().getTime();
      const difference = targetDate - now;

      if (difference <= 0) {
        document.getElementById('days').innerText = '00';
        document.getElementById('hours').innerText = '00';
        document.getElementById('minutes').innerText = '00';
        document.getElementById('seconds').innerText = '00';
        clearInterval(timerInterval);
        return;
      }

      // Calculations for days, hours, minutes and seconds
      const days = Math.floor(difference / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
      const hours = Math.floor((difference % (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) / (1000 * 60 * 60));
      const minutes = Math.floor((difference % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60));
      const seconds = Math.floor((difference % (1000 * 60)) / 1000);

      // Display results with leading zero if needed
      document.getElementById('days').innerText = days < 10 ? '0' + days : days;
      document.getElementById('hours').innerText = hours < 10 ? '0' + hours : hours;
      document.getElementById('minutes').innerText = minutes < 10 ? '0' + minutes : minutes;
      document.getElementById('seconds').innerText = seconds < 10 ? '0' + seconds : seconds;
    }

    // Initial call and set interval
    updateCountdown();
    const timerInterval = setInterval(updateCountdown, 1000);
  </script>
</body>
</html>

FILE: .\README.md (71 lines)
================================================================================
# 🐾 WhiskerWash

> Where Happy Pets Meet Expert Care! 

**WhiskerWash** is a premium, responsive multi-page web application designed for a pet care center and pet product store. It showcases professional pet services (grooming, daycare, boarding, training, walking) and offers an integrated online product catalog for pet owners.

---

## ✨ Features

- **🏠 Interactive Homepage**: Beautiful, modern landing section with a hero banner, service overview, promotional badges, and quick links.
- **🐕 Professional Services**: Detailed, dedicated landing pages for each service:
  - Daycare & Boarding
  - Grooming & Bathing (including Nail Clipping)
  - Training & Walking
- **🛒 Product Showcase**: Dedicated catalog featuring essential pet products:
  - Premium food bowls, feeders, and treat dispensers
  - High-quality grooming brushes and rakes
  - Healthy dog biscuits and durable collars/belts
- **📝 Pet Care Blog**: Informative articles on dog health, training tips, and general pet wellbeing.
- **💬 Testimonials**: Real-world reviews from happy pet owners.
- **📞 Contact & Booking**: Integrated, user-friendly contact form with validation for booking inquiries.

---

## 🛠️ Tech Stack

- **Structure**: Semantic HTML5
- **Styling**: Modern, responsive Vanilla CSS
- **Interactions**: Vanilla JavaScript for forms, validations, and interactive components

---

## 📁 Repository Structure

```text
WhiskerWash/
├── assets/
│   ├── css/          # Dedicated stylesheets for pages and layout sections
│   ├── images/       # High-quality pet and product images/assets
│   └── js/           # JavaScript files (form validation, interactive components)
├── blog/             # Blog posts and articles
├── products/         # Detailed product description pages
├── services/         # Detailed service description pages
├── homepage.html     # Website main landing page
├── about-us.html     # About the company and team
├── our-services.html # Overview of all pet care services
├── our-products.html # Product catalog index
├── testimonials.html # Customer feedback and success stories
├── contact-us.html   # Get in touch & service booking inquiries
└── coming-soon.html  # Placeholder for upcoming updates/features
```

---

## 🚀 Getting Started

### Local Setup
Since this is a client-side web application built with HTML, CSS, and JS, you can run it instantly without any build setup:

1. **Clone the repository:**
   ```bash
   git clone https://github.com/berhamindeocampo/WhiskerWash.git
   cd WhiskerWash
   ```

2. **Open the Project:**
   - Simply double-click `homepage.html` to open it in your browser.
   - Alternatively, use the **Live Server** extension in VS Code for hot-reloading development.

---

FILE: .\assets\js\contact.js (110 lines)
================================================================================
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    initCustomDropdown();
    initContactForm();
});

function initCustomDropdown() {
    const dropdown = document.getElementById('servicesDropdown');
    if (!dropdown) return;

    const toggle = dropdown.querySelector('.dropdown_toggle');
    const options = dropdown.querySelectorAll('.option_item');
    const selectedValue = dropdown.querySelector('.selected_value');
    const hiddenInput = document.getElementById('selected_service_input');

    if (!toggle || !selectedValue || !hiddenInput) return;

    toggle.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        dropdown.classList.toggle('open');
    });

    options.forEach((option) => {
        option.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();

            const value = option.getAttribute('data-value') || '';
            const text = option.textContent.trim();

            selectedValue.textContent = text;
            hiddenInput.value = value;
            selectedValue.style.color = '#111';
            dropdown.classList.remove('open');
        });
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (!dropdown.contains(e.target)) {
            dropdown.classList.remove('open');
        }
    });
}

function initContactForm() {
    const form = document.querySelector('.contact_form');
    if (!form) return;

    const popup = document.getElementById('contactPopup');
    const popupMessage = document.getElementById('contactPopupMessage');
    const closeButton = document.getElementById('closeContactPopup');

    const showPopup = (message, title = 'Message sent') => {
        if (popupMessage) popupMessage.textContent = message;
        if (popup) {
            popup.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
            popup.classList.add('open');
        }
        const titleElement = document.getElementById('contactPopupTitle');
        if (titleElement) titleElement.textContent = title;
    };

    const hidePopup = () => {
        if (popup) {
            popup.classList.remove('open');
            popup.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        }
    };

    if (closeButton) closeButton.addEventListener('click', hidePopup);
    if (popup) {
        popup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (e.target === popup) hidePopup();
        });
    }
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Escape') hidePopup();
    });

    form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        const formData = {
            name: document.getElementById('name')?.value.trim() || '',
            email: document.getElementById('email')?.value.trim() || '',
            phone: document.getElementById('phone')?.value.trim() || '',
            service: document.getElementById('selected_service_input')?.value || '',
            message: document.getElementById('message')?.value.trim() || ''
        };

        if (!formData.name || !formData.email || !formData.message) {
            showPopup('Please fill out Name, Email, and Message before sending.', 'Notice');
            return;
        }

        console.log('Form Submitted Successfully:', formData);
        showPopup('Thanks for reaching out! Your message has been captured.');

        form.reset();

        const selectedValue = document.querySelector('.selected_value');
        if (selectedValue) {
            selectedValue.textContent = 'Choose Service';
            selectedValue.style.color = '#6b7280';
        }

        const hiddenInput = document.getElementById('selected_service_input');
        if (hiddenInput) hiddenInput.value = '';
    });
}

FILE: .\assets\js\dynamic-year.js (11 lines)

const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();

const yearElement = document.getElementById('current-year');
if (yearElement) {
  yearElement.textContent = currentYear;
} else {
  const copyrightElement = document.getElementById('copyright') || document.querySelector('.copyright');
  if (copyrightElement) {
    copyrightElement.textContent = `© ${currentYear} WhiskerWash. All Rights Reserved.`;
  }
}

    <main class="blog_article">
      <section class="title_section">
        <div class="title_section_container">
          <h1>Essential Grooming Tips for<br> Your Long-Haired Cats</h1>
          <div class="blog_meta_row">
            <div class="blog_author_box">
              <img class="blog_author_image" src="../assets/images/baizhi.webp" alt="Author portrait">
              <div class="blog_author_text">
                <p class="blog_author_and_date">By Dr. Baizhi &bull; May 15, 2026</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="article_split">
        <div class="main_big_image">
          <img src="../assets/images/pexels-tima-miroshnichenko-6130977.jpg" alt="Long-haired cat being groomed">
        </div>

        <article class="blog_article_content">
        <h2>
          Mastering the Routine: How to Groom Your<br> Long-Haired Cat Without the Drama
        </h2>
         <p>
          Long-haired cats need gentle, regular grooming to keep their coats healthy, reduce tangles, and make daily care more comfortable for both the pet and the owner. When left unattended, a long, luxurious coat can quickly transform into a patchwork of painful mats that pull tightly against your cat's delicate skin. To keep their fur silky and free-flowing, consistency is key, but it requires a careful approach to avoid turning the process into a daily battlefield. Here are some essential grooming tips to help you master the routine and keep your long-haired cat looking and feeling their best.
         </p>
         <h3>
            Creating a Stress-Free Grooming Environment
         </h3>
          <p>
          The secret to a successful grooming routine lies entirely in your approach. Start with soft brushing, use cat-safe products, and keep sessions short and calm so your cat associates grooming with comfort rather than stress.
          </p>
          <p>
            Before you even bring out a comb, set the stage for success with these practical steps:
          </p>
          <ul>
            <li><strong>Choose the Right Spot:</strong> Find a quiet, comfortable area where your cat feels safe. A cozy corner with good lighting can make a big difference.</li>
            <li><strong>Use the Right Tools:</strong> Invest in a high-quality slicker brush and a wide-toothed comb designed for long-haired cats. These tools will help you gently detangle without causing discomfort.</li>
            <li><strong>Start Slow:</strong> Begin with short grooming sessions, just a few minutes at a time, and gradually increase as your cat becomes more comfortable with the process.</li>
            <h2>
              The Lifelong Benefits of Consistent Care
            </h2>
            <p>
              Pushing through the initial learning curve pays massive dividends for your cat's long-term well-being. Regular grooming not only keeps your long-haired cat looking beautiful but also promotes healthy skin and a strong bond between you and your feline friend.
            </p>
            <p>
              By taking charge of their coat maintenance, you drastically reduce the amount of loose fur they swallow during self-cleaning, effectively minimizing dangerous, uncomfortable hairballs. Furthermore, these quiet, hands-on sessions double as an excellent health checkpoint, giving you a dedicated window to spot any unusual lumps, skin irritation, or pesky parasites early on. Ultimately, turning grooming into a comforting ritual doesn't just protect their health—it transforms routine maintenance into a cherished moment of connection.
            </p>
        </article>
      </section>
    </main>

        <div class="title_section_container">
          <h1>Gentle Brushing Routines that<br> Keep Coats Silky</h1>
          <div class="blog_meta_row">
            <div class="blog_author_box">
              <img class="blog_author_image" src="../assets/images/baizhi.webp" alt="Author portrait">
              <div class="blog_author_text">
                <p class="blog_author_and_date">By Dr. Baizhi &bull; May 15, 2026</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="article_split">
        <div class="main_big_image">
          <img src="../assets/images/pexels-tima-miroshnichenko-6131568.jpg" alt="Long-haired cat being groomed">
        </div>

        <article class="blog_article_content">
        <h2>
          Mastering the Art of Brushing: Gentle Routines for Silky Coats
        </h2>
         <p>
            When you look at a long-haired cat with a majestic, flowing coat, it's easy to admire the aesthetic. But behind that gorgeous fluff lies a delicate ecosystem. Unlike short-haired breeds, a long coat is highly prone to catching debris, building up static, and forming tight mats right at the skin level. Achieving that signature, glossy sheen isn't about vigorous brushing; it's about a patient, intentional routine. Let's dive into the core practices that will transform your cat's coat from a tangled chore into a silky masterpiece.
         </p>
         <h3>
          The Gentle Brushing Routine: A Step-by-Step Guide
         </h3>
          <p>
            You wouldn't use a fine-tooth comb to untangle a thick wool sweater, and the same logic applies to your pets. Using the wrong brush can yank on the skin, creating an immediate dislike for grooming. Instead, opt for tools designed to glide through long fur without causing discomfort. Here's how to approach it:
          </p>
          <ul>
            <li><strong>The Wide-Tooth Steel Comb:</strong> Your absolute best friend. It penetrates deep into the undercoat to gently separate strands without tearing the fur.</li>
            <li><strong> The Slicker Brush:</strong> Ideal for the top layer. Its fine, angled Swire bristles are perfect for gathering loose, dead hair and smoothing out the surface to catch the light.</li>
            <li><strong>Bristle Brushes:</strong> Perfect for finishing the session. Soft, natural bristles help distribute your cat's natural skin oils evenly along the hair shafts, giving it that natural, healthy shine.</li>
          </ul>
            <h2>
              Why Regular Grooming Matters
            </h2>
            <p>
              Regular grooming not only keeps your long-haired cat looking beautiful but also promotes their overall health and well-being. By taking charge of their coat maintenance, you drastically reduce the amount of loose fur they swallow during self-cleaning, effectively minimizing dangerous, uncomfortable hairballs. Furthermore, these quiet, hands-on sessions double as an excellent health checkpoint, giving you a dedicated window to spot any unusual lumps, skin irritation, or pesky parasites early on. Ultimately, turning grooming into a comforting ritual doesn't just protect their health—it transforms routine maintenance into a cherished moment of connection.
            </p>
            <p>
              By following these gentle brushing routines and creating a stress-free grooming environment, you can help ensure that your long-haired cat's coat remains silky, healthy, and free of tangles. Regular grooming not only enhances their appearance but also strengthens the bond between you and your feline companion.
            </p>
        </article>
      </section>
    </main>

          <div class="blog_meta_row">
            <div class="blog_author_box">
              <img class="blog_author_image" src="../assets/images/baizhi.webp" alt="Author portrait">

        <h2>
          Nourishing Treats for Shiny Coats and Happy Tummies
        </h2>
         <p>
            When it comes to keeping your pet's coat glossy and their tummy happy, the right snacks can make all the difference. A diet rich in essential nutrients not only promotes a healthy coat but also supports overall well-being. Here are some delicious and nutritious snack ideas that will have your feline friend purring with delight:
          </p>
          <ul>
            <li><strong>Salmon Bites:</strong> Packed with omega-3 fatty acids, salmon is a fantastic choice for promoting a shiny coat and healthy skin. Look for treats made with real salmon for maximum benefits.</li>
            <li><strong>Chicken Jerky:</strong> High in protein and low in fat, chicken jerky is a great option for cats who need a boost of energy. Just make sure to choose treats that are free from artificial additives.</li>
            <li><strong>Catnip-Infused Treats:</strong> Not only do these treats provide a tasty snack, but they also offer the added benefit of catnip, which can help reduce stress and promote relaxation.</li>
          </ul>
         </p>
         <h3>
          Moderation is Key: Treating Your Pet Right
         </h3>
          <p>
              Remember, while treats can be a wonderful addition to your pet's diet, they should be given in moderation. Always consult with your veterinarian before introducing new snacks to ensure they are appropriate for your cat's specific dietary needs and health conditions.
            </p>

          <div class="blog_meta_row">
            <div class="blog_author_box">
              <img class="blog_author_image" src="../assets/images/baizhi.webp" alt="Author portrait">
              <div class="blog_author_text">
                <p class="blog_author_and_date">By Dr. Baizhi &bull; May 20, 2026</p>
              </div>

          <img src="../assets/images/dogcaressing.png" alt="Long-haired cat being groomed">
        </div>

        <article class="blog_article_content">
        <h2>
          Create a Calm-at-Home Spa Day Your Pet Will Love
        </h2>
         <p>
            A calm-at-home spa day can be a wonderful way to pamper your pet and strengthen your bond. Here are some tips to create a relaxing and enjoyable experience for your furry friend:
          </p>
          <ul>
            <li><strong>Set the Mood:</strong> Create a serene environment by playing soft music, dimming the lights, and using calming scents like lavender (ensure they are pet-safe).</li>
            <li><strong>Gentle Grooming:</strong> Use a soft brush to gently groom your pet's coat. This not only helps remove loose fur but also promotes relaxation through gentle touch.</li>
            <li><strong>Warm Baths:</strong> If your pet enjoys baths, use lukewarm water and a pet-friendly shampoo. Make sure to rinse thoroughly and dry them gently with a towel.</li>
            <li><strong>Massage Time:</strong> A gentle massage can help soothe your pet's muscles and promote relaxation. Focus on areas like the neck, shoulders, and back.</li>
            <li><strong>Tasty Treats:</strong> Reward your pet with their favorite treats throughout the spa day to create positive associations with the experience.</li>
          </ul>
         </p>
         <h3>
          Moderation is Key: Treating Your Pet Right
         </h3>
          <p>
              Remember, while a spa day can be a wonderful way to pamper your pet, it's important to keep their comfort and preferences in mind. Always observe their reactions and adjust the activities accordingly. If your pet shows signs of stress or discomfort, it's best to stop and try again another time. The goal is to create a positive and enjoyable experience for both you and your furry friend.
            </p>
        </article>
      </section>

      <section class="title_section">
        <div class="title_section_container">
          <h1>A Simple Wellness Checklist for Active Pets</h1>
          <div class="blog_meta_row">
            <div class="blog_author_box">
              <img class="blog_author_image" src="../assets/images/baizhi.webp" alt="Author portrait">
              <div class="blog_author_text">
                <p class="blog_author_and_date">By Dr. Baizhi &bull; May 24, 2026</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="article_split">
        <div class="main_big_image">
          <img src="../assets/images/vetconsulting.png" alt="Veterinarian examining a dog">
        </div>

        <article class="blog_article_content">
        <h2>
          A Simple Wellness Checklist for Active Pets
        </h2>
         <p>
            Keeping your active pet healthy and happy requires attention to their physical and mental well-being. Here's a simple checklist to help you ensure your furry friend is thriving in all aspects of their life:
          </p>
          <ul>
            <li><strong>Regular Exercise:</strong> Ensure your pet gets daily exercise appropriate for their breed and age. This can include walks, playtime, or interactive toys to keep them physically active.</li>
            <li><strong>Balanced Diet:</strong> Provide a nutritious diet that meets your pet's specific needs. Consult with your veterinarian to determine the best food options for your pet's health and activity level.</li>
            <li><strong>Hydration:</strong> Always have fresh water available for your pet, especially after exercise, to keep them hydrated and support overall health.</li>
            <li><strong>Routine Vet Check-ups:</strong> Schedule regular veterinary visits to monitor your pet's health, address any concerns, and stay up-to-date on vaccinations and preventive care.</li>
            <li><strong>Mental Stimulation:</strong> Engage your pet in activities that challenge their mind, such as puzzle toys, training sessions, or new experiences to keep them mentally sharp.</li>
         </p>
         <h3>
          Moderation is Key: Treating Your Pet Right
         </h3>
          <p>
              Remember, while a wellness checklist can help guide you in caring for your active pet, it's important to tailor it to their individual needs and preferences. Always observe your pet's behavior and adjust their routine accordingly to ensure they are happy, healthy, and thriving in their active lifestyle.
            </p>
        </article>
      </section>
    </main>

Professional grooming services go beyond just cutting fur; they provide a comprehensive care experience that enhances your pet's overall well-being. Here's why investing in professional grooming is beneficial for your furry friend:
          </p>
          <ul>
            <li><strong>Expert Care:</strong> Professional groomers have the knowledge and experience to handle different coat types and skin conditions safely and effectively.</li>

            <li><strong>Stress Relief:</strong> Many pets find grooming sessions relaxing, especially when done by experienced professionals who understand their body language and comfort levels.</li>
            <li><strong>Enhanced Appearance:</strong> Regular professional grooming keeps your pet's coat clean, well-maintained, and looking its best for special occasions or daily life.</li>
